## Installation & Usage
### pip install

If the python package is hosted on a repository, you can install directly using:

```sh
pip install git+https://github.com/GIT_USER_ID/GIT_REPO_ID.git
```
(you may need to run `pip` with root permission: `sudo pip install git+https://github.com/GIT_USER_ID/GIT_REPO_ID.git`)


(or `sudo python setup.py install` to install the package for all users)

## Getting Started

Please follow the [installation procedure](#installation--usage) and then run the following:

```python

import microlab_fortinet
from microlab_fortinet.rest import ApiException
from pprint import pprint

# Defining the host is optional and defaults to https://172.16.177.65/api/v2/cmdb

configuration = microlab_fortinet.Configuration(
    host = "https://172.16.177.65/api/v2/cmdb"
)

# The client must configure the authentication and authorization parameters
# in accordance with the API server security policy.
# Examples for each auth method are provided below, use the example that
# satisfies your auth use case.

# Configure API key authorization: APIKeyQueryParam
configuration.api_key['APIKeyQueryParam'] = os.environ["API_KEY"]

# Uncomment below to setup prefix (e.g. Bearer) for API key, if needed
# configuration.api_key_prefix['APIKeyQueryParam'] = 'Bearer'


# Enter a context with an instance of the API client
async with microlab_fortinet.ApiClient(configuration) as api_client:
    # Create an instance of the API class
    api_instance = microlab_fortinet.SwitchController8021XSettingsApi(api_client)

    try:
        # Select all entries in a CLI table.  Access Group: wifi
        api_response = await api_instance.switch_controller8021_x_settings_get()
        print("The response of SwitchController8021XSettingsApi->switch_controller8021_x_settings_get:\n")
        pprint(api_response)
    except ApiException as e:
        print("Exception when calling SwitchController8021XSettingsApi->switch_controller8021_x_settings_get: %s\n" % e)

```
